# The journey as a text adventure (learner child)

Everything the game said and everything the child did, level by level, in play order. Times are game seconds.

## Ninja Training (tutorial)

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja training! First, tap the big gong!
   0.4s   --- tut-gong ---
   0.5s   > child taps: gong
   0.9s   [gong]
   0.9s SENSEI: Stuck? Tap me, down here in the corner. Try it now!
   1.7s   --- tut-help ---
   1.7s   [great]
   1.7s   > child taps: Help
   1.8s SENSEI: That's it! I'm always here to help.
   4.2s   > child taps: Help
   4.5s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   5.4s   --- tut-speaker ---
   5.4s   > child taps: Hear it again
   5.4s   [good]
   5.4s SENSEI: And when you tap the speaker, I'll say it again!
   7.9s   > child taps: Hear it again
   8.4s SENSEI: Welcome to Bamboo Village!
  10.5s SENSEI: Tap the glowing stone to start your next adventure.

## Show Sensei (placement)

   0.0s SENSEI: Let's see what you know! Just have a go.
   0.8s   --- find ---
   0.8s   > child taps: s
   0.9s   [good]
   1.7s SENSEI: Which is the spelling of this sound?
   2.1s   [good]
   2.1s   > child taps: p
   3.3s   > child taps: i
   3.3s SENSEI: Brilliant!
   4.5s SENSEI: Which one is...
      🔊 "at"
   7.0s   [good]
   7.0s   > child taps: at
   7.9s SENSEI: Three right answers in a row! Your ninja is getting stronger.
   9.4s   > child taps: at
  10.6s   > child taps: an
  11.5s SENSEI: Which one is...
      🔊 "am"
  14.2s   [good]
  14.2s   > child taps: am
  15.3s SENSEI: Brilliant!
  16.4s SENSEI: Which one is...
  16.7s   [good]
  16.7s   > child taps: cap
      🔊 "cap"
  17.9s SENSEI: Which one is...
  17.9s   [wrong]
  17.9s   > child taps: set
  18.6s   [fanfare]
  18.6s SENSEI: Wow! Now I know just where to start your adventure. Let's go!
  19.5s   [great]

## Bamboo Village w1-wu1: ears

   0.0s SENSEI: Ninja ears on! Let's listen to some words.
   0.4s   --- warmup ---
   3.2s SENSEI: This is the sun.
   5.0s SENSEI: This is a sock.
   6.2s SENSEI: This is a cat.
   7.5s SENSEI: Let me show you!
   8.7s SENSEI: Tap the sun!
   9.7s   [good]
  10.2s SENSEI: Now you try!
  11.1s SENSEI: Tap the sock!
  11.4s   > child taps: sock
  11.4s   [good]
  12.0s SENSEI: Watch me first!
  13.4s SENSEI: I can say a word fast. Sun!
  15.4s SENSEI: Or I can say it slowly...
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  19.7s SENSEI: Fast or slow, it's the same word. Sun!
  23.0s SENSEI: Slowly, I hear its sounds. Words are made of sounds!
  27.5s SENSEI: Your turn! Tap the tortoise, and say it slowly with me.
  28.4s   > child taps: tortoise
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  31.2s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  32.1s   > child taps: sun
      🔊 "sun" (slowly)
  34.5s SENSEI: Now tap the rabbit, and say it fast!
  36.9s   > child taps: rabbit
      🔊 "sun"
  37.9s SENSEI: Listen to the very first sound.
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held) · "sock" (first sound held)
  42.5s SENSEI: Did you notice? Sun and sock start with the same sound...
      🔊 /s/
  47.7s SENSEI: Say that sound with me!
      🔊 /s/
  51.5s   > child taps: Next
  51.9s SENSEI: This is a sausage.
  53.9s SENSEI: This is the moon.
  55.6s SENSEI: Let me show you!
  56.7s SENSEI: Tap all the pictures that start with...
      🔊 /s/
  60.5s   [good]
      🔊 "sun" (first sound held)
  61.7s SENSEI: Your turn!
  62.5s   > child taps: sock
  62.5s   [good]
      🔊 "sock" (first sound held)
  65.0s   > child taps: sun
  68.6s   > child taps: sausage
  68.6s   [good]
      🔊 "sausage" (first sound held)
  70.8s SENSEI: You found them both! They both start with...
      🔊 /s/
  74.8s   [great]
  74.8s SENSEI: You can hear the sounds in words. Brilliant listening!
  76.3s   [great]
  78.6s   [gong]
  78.7s SENSEI: Look! Your pictures are turning into stickers!
  79.6s   --- stickers ---
  82.4s SENSEI: This is your Sticker Book!
  83.9s SENSEI: Sun, sock, cat, sausage and moon!
  88.9s SENSEI: Every picture you play with becomes a sticker!
  91.6s SENSEI: Tap a sticker!
  91.8s   > child taps: sticker sun
      🔊 "sun" · "sun" (slowly)
  94.2s   > child taps: sticker sun
  96.3s SENSEI: Tap the green arrow to carry on!
